Let's think of algorithms:
- Use parametric representation like low-order spline or Fourier/amplitude constraints to reduce dimension and improve precision? Discrete n freely, evaluate objective exact discrete. We can optimize in continuous differentiable domain with smooth max. But discrete max of quadratic is nonsmooth; local solvers may stall.
- Use min-max quadratic programming: minimize t subject to h∈[0,1], sum=n/2, q_k(h)=Σ h_i(1-h_{i±k}) ≤ t, for k = 0..n-1. This is nonconvex due quadratic. But can formulate q_k = linear - h^T T_k h. Minimize max linear - quadratic. Hard.
- However using g: c_k = (N_k - g^T T_k g) dx/4. Constraints g^T T_k g ≥ N_k(1-4C?) in terms of C? Actually t objective: c_k ≤t => g^T T_k g ≥ N_k - 4t n/2? Let's derive: c_k dx = (Σ(1-gg))/4 dx = (N_k - R_k)*dx/4. dx=2/n => c=(N_k - R_k)/(2n). Constraint c≤t => R_k ≥ N_k - 2 n t. For t=0.38, threshold=N_k -0.76n. For k=0: N=n, R_0≥0.24n. R_0=Σg_i^2. Since g in [-1,1], possible. Linear? Quadratic lower bounds plus box and mean zero. This is perspective? Not convex because PSD lower bound? g^T T_k g is quadratic indefinite for nonzero k (Toeplitz with - on anti-diagonal? Could be indefinite).
- Could use lcp? No.

Could enforce sum by selecting one value as target - sum others, then if clip causes issue. We can do:
```
def ensure_sum(h,n):
    target=n/2.0
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    for _ in range(5):
       s=np.sum(h)
       diff=target-s
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       # adjust element with largest slack
       slack=np.minimum(h,1-h)
       idx=np.argmax(slack)
       h[idx]+=diff
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # final adjust last in a way that if within bounds
    if abs(np.sum(h)-target)>1e-14:
       slack=...; idx=argmax
       h[idx]=min(max(h[idx]+(target-np.sum(h)),0),1)
    return h
```

SLSQP top-level with args:
```
def slsqp_obj(x, n, beta):
    c=compute_corr(x)
    return smooth_max(c,beta)*(2.0/n)
def slsqp_jac(x,n,beta):
    c=compute_corr(x)
    w=smooth_weights(c,beta)
    g=corr_grad_weights(x,w,n)
    return (2.0/n)*g
```

Pseudo:
```
def refine_with_slsqp(h0,n,start,budget):
   h=project_feasible(h0,n); c=compute_c5(h)
   betas=[30,80,200,500,1000,2000]
   for beta in betas:
      rem=remaining_time(start,budget,20)
      if rem<25: break
      maxiter=200 if beta<100 else 300
      try: h1,c1=run_slsqp(h,n,beta,maxiter)
      except: continue
      h=project_feasible(h1,n); c=compute_c5(h)
   return h,c
```

Run_slsqp top-level:
```
def run_slsqp(h0,n,beta,maxiter):
    h0=project_feasible(h0,n)
    bounds=[(0.,1.)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_con,'jac':sum_jac}]
    res = minimize(slsqp_obj, h0, jac=slsqp_jac, args=(n,beta), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'iprint':0})
    h=project_feasible(res.x,n)
    return h, compute_c5(h)
```
Need import minimize at top.

Potential SLSQP failure with equality and bounds if h0 not exactly feasible; project okay. `sum_con` returns `np.sum(x) - len(x)/2.0`.

Could use `method='trust-constr'` optionally if SLSQP not improving? Maybe not.

def trust_jac...
cons = LinearConstraint([[1,..,1]], [n/2],[n/2]) from scipy.optimize
res=minimize(trust_obj, h, jac=trust_jac, method='trust-constr', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'gtol':1e-12,'verbose':0})
```

Exact local active line search:
Implement `projected_gradient_polish(h,n,start,budget, beta=500, iters=2000)`:
```
def polish_gradient(h,n,start,budget):
   h=project_feasible(h,n); F=compute_c5(h)
   d_prev=np.zeros(n); step=0.05
   for it in range(iters):
      if remaining_time<5: break
      corr=compute_corr(h); M=max; w=smooth_weights(corr,beta)
      g=corr_grad_weights(h,w,n) * (2/n)
      g -= np.mean(g)
      d = 0.9*d_prev + g
      d -= np.mean(d)
      norm=np.linalg.norm(d)
      if norm<1e-12: break
      d = d/norm
      improved=False
      for try_step in [step, step*0.5, step*0.2, step*0.05, step*2?]:
          cand=project_feasible(h - try_step*d, n)
          F1=compute_c5(cand)
          if F1 < F - 1e-12:
              h=cand; F=F1; improved=True; step=min(0.2, step*1.2)
              break
          else:
              step*=0.5
      if not improved:
          step*=0.3
          if step<1e-5: break
      d_prev=d
   return h,F
```
This may be slow with compute_corr each step (200*40k=8M over 200 steps okay). Use max 500. Since gradient of smooth may not align exact; line search exact. Could improve.

def local_pair_search(h,n,start,budget, max_rounds=10):
   h=project_feasible(h,n); C=compute_c5(h)
   for round in range(max_rounds):
      if remaining<10 break
      corr=compute_corr(h); M=max; index list active = argwhere(corr > M - max(1e-5, 1e-6*M)) maybe top 20.
      pairs=set()
      for idx in active_indices:
         L=idx-(n-1)
         if L>0:
            # c=Σ_i h_{i+L}(1-h_i), pairs (i, i+L)
            for i in range(n-L): add (i,i+L)
         elif L<0: Lp=-L; for i in range(Lp,n): add (i-Lp,i)? maybe pair (i-Lp,i)
         else: pairs? Can't pair zero; use random? For L=0, c_i=h_i(1-h_i); pair? Move between i and j? Maybe skip or use top variables.
      limit pairs to e.g. 300 random or by large contribution.
      improved=False
      for (i,j) in pairs:
         if timing break
         lo=max(-h[i], h[j]-1); hi=min(1-h[i], h[i]? wait h_j-δ ≥0 =>δ≤h_j; h_i+δ≤1 =>δ≤1-h_i; h_i+δ≥0 =>δ≥-h_i; h_j-δ≤1 =>δ≥h_j-1.)
         if hi-lo<1e-10: continue

Detailed functions:
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def compute_corr(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')

def compute_c5(h):
    n=len(h)
    if n==0: return 0.0
    corr=compute_corr(h)
    return float(np.max(corr) * (2.0/n))
```

`project_feasible`:
Use water-filling. Need if n==0. For target n/2.
```
def project_feasible(v,n):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=float).copy()
    target=n/2.0
    if n==0: return v
    # If v has nan, fill 0.5?
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)): v=np.where(np.isfinite(v),v,0.5)
    lo = float(np.min(v)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(v)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       h=np.clip(v-mid,0.0,1.0)
       s=np.sum(h)
       if s > target:
          lo=mid
       else:
          hi=mid
    h=np.clip(v-0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    # corrective
    for _ in range(10):
       s=np.sum(h)
       diff=target-s
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       margin=np.minimum(h,1.0-h)
       cnt=int(np.sum(margin>1e-12))
       if cnt>0:
           m=margin>1e-12
           h[m] += diff/cnt
           h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       else:
           idx=int(np.argmax(margin))
           h[idx]=np.clip(h[idx]+diff,0,1)
    # final exact tweak
    s=np.sum(h)
    diff=target-s
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       margin=np.minimum(h,1.0-h)
       idx=int(np.argmax(margin))
       h[idx]=np.clip(h[idx]+diff,0,1)
    return h
```
Potential issue: For water-filling, if target 0 or n, but our target n/2 inside. Good.

`sum_con` and jac:
```
def sum_con(x): return float(np.sum(x) - len(x)/2.0)
def sum_jac(x): return np.ones_like(x, dtype=float)
```

`smooth_max`:
```
def smooth_max(corr,beta):
    m=float(np.max(corr))
    shifted=np.clip(beta*(corr-m), -700.0, 0.0)
    # logsumexp
    total=np.sum(np.exp(shifted))
    return m + np.log(total)/beta
```
If beta large and all zero shifted, total len, log small. Good.

`smooth_weights`: similar.

`corr_grad_weights(h, weights, n)`:
```
def corr_grad_weights(h, weights, n):
    g=np.zeros(n,float)
    for k in range(2*n-1):
        w=float(weights[k])
        if w < 1e-18: continue
        L=k-(n-1)
        if L==0:
            g += w*(1.0-2.0*h)
        elif L>0:
            m=n-L
            g[L:] += w*(1.0 - h[:m])
            g[:m] -= w*h[L:]
        else:
            p=-L
            m=n-p
            g[:m] += w*(1.0 - h[p:])
            g[p:] -= w*h[:m]
    return g
```
This loops Python 2n up to 800 -> okay. But inside SLSQP many evals; optimizing gradient with vectorization? Could precompute shifts? For n=400, 800 loop, each small vector ops; maybe okay. 1500 evals*800 =1.2M vector ops, fine.

`slsqp_obj`:
```
def slsqp_obj(x,n,beta):
    corr=compute_corr(x)
    return smooth_max(corr,beta)*(2.0/n)
def slsqp_jac(x,n,beta):
    corr=compute_corr(x)
    w=smooth_weights(corr,beta)
    return corr_grad_weights(x,w,n)*(2.0/n)
```

`run_slsqp`:
```
def run_slsqp(h0,n,beta,maxiter):
    h0=project_feasible(h0,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_con,'jac':sum_jac}]
    res=minimize(slsqp_obj, h0, jac=slsqp_jac, args=(n,beta), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=constraints, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'iprint':0})
    h=project_feasible(res.x,n)
    return h, compute_c5(h)
```
Need if SLSQP diverges to infeasible; project.

`run_trust`? Maybe skip.

`interp_resample` with water projection:
```
def interp_resample(h_src,n_dst):
    n_src=len(h_src)
    if n_src==n_dst: return project_feasible(h_src.copy(),n_dst)
    dx_src=2.0/n_src; dx_dst=2.0/n_dst
    xs=(np.arange(n_src)+0.5)*dx_src
    xd=(np.arange(n_dst)+0.5)*dx_dst
    h=np.interp(xd,xs,h_src)
    return project_feasible(h,n_dst)
```

Random inits:
```
def make_random_h(n,rng):
   # smooth random: Fourier low freq
   t=np.arange(n)*2.0/n
   h=0.5
   for m in range(1,4):
       a=rng.normal(0,0.1)
       b=rng.normal(0,0.1)
       h += a*np.cos(np.pi*m*t) + b*np.sin(np.pi*m*t)
   return project_feasible(h,n)
def make_binary_random(n,rng):
   h=np.zeros(n); idx=rng.choice(n,n//2,replace=False); h[idx]=1; return h
```

Gradient polish:
```
def polish_gradient(h,n,start,budget, beta=800, max_iter=500):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    F=compute_c5(h)
    prev_dir=np.zeros(n)
    step=0.03
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if remaining_time(start,budget,6) < 2: break
       corr=compute_corr(h)
       w=smooth_weights(corr,beta)
       g=corr_grad_weights(h,w,n)*(2.0/n)
       g -= np.mean(g)
       norm=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if norm < 1e-10: break
       d = 0.5*prev_dir + g
       d -= np.mean(d)
       nd=np.linalg.norm(d)
       if nd < 1e-12:
          d=g/norm
       else:
          d=d/nd
       prev_dir=d
       improved=False
       # candidate steps
       for s in (step, step*0.5, step*0.2, step*0.05, step*1.5):
          cand=project_feasible(h - s*d, n)
          F1=compute_c5(cand)
          if F1 < F - 1e-12:
             h=cand; F=F1; improved=True
             step=min(0.1, step*1.1)
             break
          if F1 < F: # accept tiny? no
             pass
       if not improved:
          step*=0.5
          if step < 1e-5: break
    return h,F
```
This may be slowly. Use max_iter 300. It evaluates up to 5 C per iter => 1500*40k=60M, okay.

def local_pair_search(h,n,start,budget,max_rounds=6,max_pairs=120):

def binary_local(h,n,start,budget,max_moves=5000):
    h=project_feasible(h,n); h[(h>0.5).astype(float)]=...
    C=compute_c5(h)
    ones=np.where(h==1)[0]; zeros=...

    for it in range(max_moves):

    return h,C

Active binary flips: recompute active lags each round, generate pairs of (i,j) where moving 1 to 0? Let's implement pair search robust:
```
def local_pair_search(h,n,start,budget,max_rounds=10,max_pairs=250):
  h=project_feasible(h,n); C=compute_c5(h)
  for rd in range(max_rounds):
    if remaining_time(start,budget,15) < 10: break
    corr=compute_corr(h); M=max
    # active: top up to 12 lags within 1e-6 of M? or top 12
    active_idx = np.argsort(-corr)[:12]
    pairs=[]; seen=set()
    # helper add pair active top values
    for idx in active_idx:
       L=idx-(n-1)
       if L==0: continue
       if L>0:
          m=n-L; vals = h[L:] * (1-h[:m])
       else:
          p=-L; m=n-p; vals = h[:m]*(1-h[p:])
       if m <=0: continue
       take = max(1, min(m, max_pairs//len(active_idx)))
       if take < m:
          part = np.argpartition(vals, -take)[-take:]
       else: part=np.arange(m)
       # sort descending for best
       part = part[np.argsort(-vals[part])]
       for ii in part:
          if L>0: i=int(ii); j=i+L
          else: i=int(ii); j=i+(-L)
          if i==j: continue
          key=(min(i,j),max(i,j))
          if key in seen: continue
          seen.add(key); pairs.append((i,j))
          if len(pairs)>=max_pairs: break
       if len(pairs)>=max_pairs: break
    # Add random high-low pairs if not enough:
    if len(pairs) < max_pairs:
       high_idx=np.argsort(-h)[:20]; low_idx=np.argsort(h)[:20]
       rng=np.random.RandomState(1000+rd)
       count=0
       while len(pairs)<max_pairs and count<200:
          i=int(low_idx[rng.randint(len(low_idx))]); j=int(high_idx[rng.randint(len(high_idx))]); count+=1
          if i==j: continue
          key=(min(i,j),max(i,j)); if key in seen: continue
          seen.add(key); pairs.append((i,j))
    if not pairs: break
    improved=False
    # Evaluate pairs; maybe randomize order to escape? Keep descending.
    for (i,j) in pairs:
       if remaining_time(start,budget,15) < 3: break
       # Ensure i is maybe the one to increase? We sample both directions so no matter.
       lo=max(-h[i], h[j]-1.0); hi=min(1.0-h[i], h[j])
       if hi-lo < 1e-9: continue
       # sample grid
       best_val=0.0; bestF=C
       # include 0
       deltas=np.linspace(lo,hi,11)
       for val in deltas:
          v=float(val)
          ni=h[i]+v; nj=h[j]-v
          if ni<0 or ni>1 or nj<0 or nj>1: continue
          h2=h.copy(); h2[i]=ni; h2[j]=nj
          # Because sum slightly, adjust? Not needed but can if abs sum >1e-12.
          F2=compute_c5(h2)
          if F2 < bestF:
             bestF=F2; best_val=v
       if bestF < C - 1e-12:
          h[i]=float(h[i]+best_val); h[j]=float(h[j]-best_val)
          h=project_feasible(h,n)
          C=compute_c5(h)
          improved=True
       if len(pairs)>50 and time too: maybe break? Use remaining.
    if not improved: break
  return h,C
```
This treats pairs unordered: If pair is (low, high) and we move δ from first to second. Sampling both positive/negative covers both directions: δ positive increases i, decreases j; negative opposite. Good.

We can enforce `if abs(np.sum(h2)-n/2)>1e-12: h2=project_feasible(h2,n)`. But project may change pair; still okay. To save, skip; C for slightly infeasible may be invalid. Better ensure h is feasible before search. After each accept project. Floating sum error small.

`project_feasible` inside every accept (maybe many) can be costly but okay; only when improved. Could recompute C after project. If projection changes values, the δ move maybe not exactly the best, but still likely improves.

```
def round_to_binary(h,n):
   target=n//2 if n%2==0 else int(np.round(n/2.0)) # not exact if odd; only even
   hr=(h>0.5).astype(float)
   cnt=sum; if cnt>target: set lowest h among ones to0; if cnt<target: set highest zeros to1.
   return hr
```
For even only.

import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def remaining_time(start, budget_s, reserve):
    return max(0.0, budget_s - (time.time() - start) - reserve)

def compute_corr(h): ...

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
   start=time.time()
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   best_h=None; best_c=1e9; best_n=0
   try:
      parent=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float)
      if parent.size>0: ...
   except Exception:
      pass
   if best_h is None: ...
   # initial quick pair? no
   # refine parent
   if remaining_time(start,budget_s,60)>60:
      h1,c1=refine_with_slsqp(best_h,best_n,start,budget_s)
      if c1<best_c: best...
      h2,c2=polish_gradient(h1,best_n,start,budget_s, beta=600, max_iter=200)
      if c2<best_c: best...
      h3,c3=local_pair_search(best_h,best_n,start,budget_s,max_rounds=8,max_pairs=250)
      if c3<best_c: best...
   # candidate resolutions
   n_list=...
   for n in n_list:
      if n==best_n: continue
      if remaining_time(start,budget_s,120)<120: break
      h0=interp_resample(best_h,n)
      # limit betas by time
      h1,c1=refine_with_slsqp(h0,n,start,budget_s)

      h2,c2=local_pair_search(best_h or h1,n,...)

      for ri in range(2):
         h0=make_random_h(best_n,rng)
         h1,c1=refine_with_slsqp(h0,best_n,start,budget_s, heavy=False)
         if c1<best_c: best...
         h2,c2=local_pair_search(h1,best_n,...)
         update
   # binary rounding at best_n
   if best_n%2==0 and remaining_time(start,budget_s,80)>80:
      hb=round_to_binary(best_h,best_n)
      cb=compute_c5(hb)
      if cb<best_c: best...
      hb,cb=local_pair_search(hb,best_n,start,budget_s,max_rounds=6,max_pairs=200)
      if cb<best_c: best...
   # final
   best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
   final_c=compute_c5(best_h)
   try: eval=evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h); if eval<final_c: final_c=eval
   except: pass
   return best_h, final_c, best_n

```
def refine_with_slsqp(h0,n,start,budget_s,heavy=True):
   h=project_feasible(h0,n); c=compute_c5(h); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c
   if not heavy: betas=[50,200,600]
   else:
      if n<=256: betas=[30,80,160,320,640,1200]
      elif n<=400: betas=[30,80,160,320,640]
      else: betas=[30,80,160,320]
   for beta in betas:
      rem=remaining_time(start,budget_s,30)
      if rem<30: break
      maxiter = 250 if n<=256 else 150
      if beta>=500: maxiter = min(maxiter, 300)
      try: h1,c1=run_slsqp(h,n,beta,maxiter)
      except: break
      h1=project_feasible(h1,n); c1=compute_c5(h1)
      # update best
      if c1<best_c: best_h=h1.copy(); best_c=c1
      # decide current
      if c1 <= c + 0.002:
         h=h1; c=c1
      else:
         # keep old current but maybe set h=h1 if smooth? revert
         pass
   return best_h,best_c

There is a trick: after final, choose an index with slack and set it to `target - sum(h[other indices])` computed using the same sum order? `np.sum` uses pairwise summation; if we set h[idx] = target - np.sum(np.delete(h,idx)), then full `np.sum(h)` may not exactly equal due summation order, but likely close. We can do:
```
def force_exact_sum(h,n):
   target=n/2.0
   for _ in range(3):
       s=np.sum(h)
       diff=target-s
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       # adjust a coordinate with max slack by diff, but if diff too large, split
       slack=np.minimum(h,1-h)
       idx=int(np.argmax(slack))
       h[idx]=float(np.clip(h[idx]+diff,0,1))
   return h
```

`round_to_binary`: For n even. If target int. If count adjust:
```
def round_to_binary(h,n):
    target=n//2
    hr=(h>0.5).astype(float)
    cnt=int(np.sum(hr))
    if cnt>target:
       ones=np.where(hr==1.0)[0]
       order=np.argsort(h[ones]) # lowest h to drop? Since h was >0.5, to round best, drop smallest original h.
       for idx in ones[order[:cnt-target]]: hr[idx]=0
    elif cnt<target:
       zeros=np.where(hr==0.0)[0]
       order=np.argsort(-h[zeros]) # promote largest
       for idx in zeros[order[:target-cnt]]: hr[idx]=1
    return project_feasible(hr,n)
```
If n odd, skip.

def refine_with_slsqp(h0, n, start, budget_s, heavy=True):
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    c = compute_c5(h)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c=c
    if heavy:
       if n <= 224:
          ladder = ((40,120), (100,160), (240,200), (600,240), (1500,280))
       elif n <= 350:
          ladder = ((40,100), (120,130), (300,170), (800,200))
       else:
          ladder = ((40,80), (150,110), (400,140))
    else:
       ladder = ((50,80), (200,110), (600,140))
    for beta, maxiter in ladder:
       rem=remaining_time(start,budget_s,35)
       if rem < 35: break
       # scale maxiter by remaining? maybe if rem < 60: maxiter=min(maxiter,60)
       if rem < 70: maxiter=min(maxiter,60)
       try:
           h1,c1=run_slsqp(h,n,beta,maxiter)
       except Exception:
           break
       h1=project_feasible(h1,n); c1=compute_c5(h1)
       if c1 < best_c - 1e-12:
           best_h=h1.copy(); best_c=c1
       if c1 <= c + 0.0015:
           h=h1; c=c1
       else:
           # if significant worse, keep current, but maybe still try next beta from current
           pass
    # If best_h differs from h? Return best_h
    return best_h,best_c
```

`polish_gradient` top-level:
```
def polish_gradient(h,n,start,budget_s,beta=600,max_iter=150):
    h=project_feasible(h,n); F=compute_c5(h); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=F
    prev=np.zeros(n); step=0.02
    for it in range(max_iter):
       rem=remaining_time(start,budget_s,10)
       if rem<5: break
       corr=compute_corr(h)
       w=smooth_weights(corr,beta)
       g=corr_grad_weights(h,w,n)*(2.0/n)
       g-=np.mean(g)
       ng=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if ng<1e-12: break
       d=0.6*prev + g/ng
       d-=np.mean(d)
       nd=np.linalg.norm(d)
       if nd<1e-12: d=g/ng
       else: d/=nd
       prev=d.copy()
       improved=False
       for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.2,0.05,2.0,1.5):
          s=step*mult
          if s<=0: continue
          cand=project_feasible(h - s*d,n)
          F1=compute_c5(cand)
          if F1 < F - 1e-12:
              h=cand; F=F1; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=F; improved=True
              step=min(0.08, step*1.15)
              break
       if not improved:
          step*=0.4
          if step<1e-5: break
       if it % 10 == 9:
          # Add small random kick?
          pass
    return best_h,best_c
```
If best_h updated but current h maybe same. Return best.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def remaining_time(start, budget_s, reserve):
    return max(0.0, budget_s - (time.time() - start) - reserve)


def compute_corr(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def compute_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    corr = compute_corr(h)
    return float(np.max(corr) * (2.0 / n))


def adjust_sum(h, n):
    target = n / 2.0
    for _ in range(16):
        s = float(np.sum(h))
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        margin = np.minimum(h, 1.0 - h)
        cnt = int(np.sum(margin > 1e-10))
        if cnt > 0:
            m = margin > 1e-10
            h[m] += diff / cnt
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(margin))
            h[idx] = float(np.clip(h[idx] + diff, 0.0, 1.0))
    return h


def project_feasible(v, n):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if v.size != n:
        v = np.resize(v, n)
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.5)
    h = np.clip(v.copy(), 0.0, 1.0)
    target = n / 2.0
    s = float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(s - target) <= 1e-12:
        return adjust_sum(h, n)
    if abs(s - target) < 0.5:
        h += (target - s) / n
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        return adjust_sum(h, n)
    lo = float(np.min(v)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(v)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(90):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        h = np.clip(v - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        s = float(np.sum(h))
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    h = np.clip(v - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    return adjust_sum(h, n)


def sum_con(x):
    return float(np.sum(x) - len(x) / 2.0)


def sum_jac(x):
    return np.ones_like(x, dtype=float)


def smooth_max(corr, beta):
    m = float(np.max(corr))
    shifted = np.clip(beta * (corr - m), -700.0, 0.0)
    total = float(np.sum(np.exp(shifted)))
    return m + np.log(total) / beta


def smooth_weights(corr, beta):
    m = float(np.max(corr))
    shifted = np.clip(beta * (corr - m), -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s > 0:
        return e / s
    return np.full_like(corr, 1.0 / len(corr))


def corr_grad_weights(h, weights, n):
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for k in range(2 * n - 1):
        w = float(weights[k])
        if w < 1e-18:
            continue
        L = k - (n - 1)
        if L == 0:
            g += w * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        elif L > 0:
            m = n - L
            if m <= 0:
                continue
            g[L:] += w * (1.0 - h[:m])
            g[:m] -= w * h[L:]
        else:
            p = -L
            m = n - p
            if m <= 0:
                continue
            g[:m] += w * (1.0 - h[p:])
            g[p:] -= w * h[:m]
    return g


def slsqp_obj(x, n, beta):
    corr = compute_corr(x)
    return smooth_max(corr, beta) * (2.0 / n)


def slsqp_jac(x, n, beta):
    corr = compute_corr(x)
    w = smooth_weights(corr, beta)
    return corr_grad_weights(x, w, n) * (2.0 / n)


def run_slsqp(h0, n, beta, maxiter):
    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": sum_con, "jac": sum_jac}]
    try:
        res = minimize(
            slsqp_obj,
            h0,
            jac=slsqp_jac,
            args=(n, beta),
            method="SLSQP",
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-12, "iprint": 0},
        )
    except Exception:
        return project_feasible(h0, n), compute_c5(h0)
    if hasattr(res, "x") and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
        h = project_feasible(res.x, n)
    else:
        h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    return h, compute_c5(h)


def refine_with_slsqp(h0, n, start, budget_s, heavy=True):
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    c = compute_c5(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c

    if heavy:
        if n <= 224:
            ladder = ((40, 120), (100, 160), (240, 200), (600, 240), (1500, 280))
        elif n <= 350:
            ladder = ((40, 100), (120, 130), (300, 170), (800, 200))
        else:
            ladder = ((40, 80), (150, 110), (400, 140))
    else:
        ladder = ((50, 80), (200, 110), (600, 140))

    for beta, maxiter in ladder:
        rem = remaining_time(start, budget_s, 35)
        if rem < 35:
            break
        use_iter = maxiter
        if rem < 70:
            use_iter = min(use_iter, 35)
        try:
            h1, c1 = run_slsqp(h, n, beta, use_iter)
        except Exception:
            break
        h1 = project_feasible(h1, n)
        c1 = compute_c5(h1)
        if c1 < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_h = h1.copy()
            best_c = c1
        if c1 <= c + 0.0015:
            h = h1
            c = c1
    return best_h, best_c


def interp_resample(h_src, n_dst):
    n_src = len(h_src)
    if n_src == n_dst:
        return project_feasible(h_src.copy(), n_dst)
    xs = (np.arange(n_src) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_src)
    xd = (np.arange(n_dst) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_dst)
    h = np.interp(xd, xs, h_src)
    return project_feasible(h, n_dst)


def make_random_h(n, rng):
    t = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    h = np.full(n, 0.5)
    for m in range(1, 4):
        a = rng.normal(0.0, 0.12)
        b = rng.normal(0.0, 0.12)
        h += a * np.cos(np.pi * m * t) + b * np.sin(np.pi * m * t)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def make_binary_random(h0, rng):
    n = len(h0)
    target = int(round(n / 2.0))
    h = (h0 > 0.5).astype(float)
    cnt = int(np.sum(h))
    if cnt > target:
        ones = np.where(h == 1.0)[0]
        order = np.argsort(h0[ones])
        for idx in ones[order[: cnt - target]]:
            h[idx] = 0.0
    elif cnt < target:
        zeros = np.where(h == 0.0)[0]
        order = np.argsort(-h0[zeros])
        for idx in zeros[order[: target - cnt]]:
            h[idx] = 1.0
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def polish_gradient(h, n, start, budget_s, beta=700, max_iter=160):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    F = compute_c5(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = F
    prev = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    step = 0.02

    for _ in range(max_iter):
        rem = remaining_time(start, budget_s, 10)
        if rem < 5:
            break
        corr = compute_corr(h)
        w = smooth_weights(corr, beta)
        g = corr_grad_weights(h, w, n) * (2.0 / n)
        g -= np.mean(g)
        ng = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if ng < 1e-12:
            break
        d = 0.6 * prev + g / ng
        d -= np.mean(d)
        nd = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if nd < 1e-12:
            d = g / ng
        else:
            d = d / nd
        prev = d.copy()

        improved = False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.2, 0.05, 2.0, 1.5):
            s = step * mult
            if s <= 0:
                continue
            cand = project_feasible(h - s * d, n)
            F1 = compute_c5(cand)
            if F1 < F - 1e-12:
                h = cand
                F = F1
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_c = F
                improved = True
                step = min(0.08, step * 1.15)
                break
        if not improved:
            step *= 0.4
            if step < 1e-5:
                break
    return best_h, best_c


def local_pair_search(h, n, start, budget_s, max_rounds=8, max_pairs=250):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    C = compute_c5(h)

    for rd in range(max_rounds):
        if remaining_time(start, budget_s, 18) < 8:
            break
        corr = compute_corr(h)
        M = float(np.max(corr))

        active = np.argsort(-corr)
        active_idx = []
        for idx in active[:14]:
            idx = int(idx)
            if corr[idx] >= M - max(1e-8, 1e-8 * max(1.0, M)):
                active_idx.append(idx)
            else:
                break
        if not active_idx:
            active_idx = [int(np.argmax(corr))]

        pairs = []
        seen = set()
        per_lag = max(1, max_pairs // max(1, len(active_idx)))

        for idx in active_idx:
            L = idx - (n - 1)
            if L == 0:
                continue
            if L > 0:
                m = n - L
                if m <= 0:
                    continue
                vals = h[L:] * (1.0 - h[:m])
                if len(vals) <= per_lag:
                    part = np.arange(len(vals))
                else:
                    part = np.argpartition(vals, -per_lag)[-per_lag:]
                part = part[np.argsort(-vals[part])]
                for ii in part:
                    i = int(ii)
                    j = i + L
                    key = (min(i, j), max(i, j))
                    if key in seen or i == j:
                        continue
                    seen.add(key)
                    pairs.append((i, j))
            else:
                p = -L
                m = n - p
                if m <= 0:
                    continue
                vals = h[:m] * (1.0 - h[p:])
                if len(vals) <= per_lag:
                    part = np.arange(len(vals))
                else:
                    part = np.argpartition(vals, -per_lag)[-per_lag:]
                part = part[np.argsort(-vals[part])]
                for ii in part:
                    i = int(ii)
                    j = i + p
                    key = (min(i, j), max(i, j))
                    if key in seen or i == j:
                        continue
                    seen.add(key)
                    pairs.append((i, j))
            if len(pairs) >= max_pairs:
                break

        if len(pairs) < max_pairs and n >= 10:
            high = np.argsort(-h)[:24]
            low = np.argsort(h)[:24]
            rng = np.random.RandomState(101 + rd)
            cnt = 0
            while len(pairs) < max_pairs and cnt < 500:
                i = int(low[rng.randint(len(low))])
                j = int(high[rng.randint(len(high))])
                cnt += 1
                if i == j:
                    continue
                key = (min(i, j), max(i, j))
                if key in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(key)
                pairs.append((i, j))

        if not pairs:
            break

        improved_this_round = False
        for (i, j) in pairs:
            if remaining_time(start, budget_s, 18) < 4:
                break
            if i == j:
                continue
            lo = max(-h[i], h[j] - 1.0)
            hi = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
            if hi - lo < 1e-10:
                continue

            best_val = 0.0
            bestF = C
            deltas = np.linspace(lo, hi, 11)
            for val in deltas:
                v = float(val)
                ni = h[i] + v
                nj = h[j] - v
                if ni < 0.0 - 1e-14 or ni > 1.0 + 1e-14:
                    continue
                if nj < 0.0 - 1e-14 or nj > 1.0 + 1e-14:
                    continue
                ni = float(np.clip(ni, 0.0, 1.0))
                nj = float(np.clip(nj, 0.0, 1.0))
                h2 = h.copy()
                h2[i] = ni
                h2[j] = nj
                F2 = compute_c5(h2)
                if F2 < bestF:
                    bestF = F2
                    best_val = v

            if bestF < C - 1e-12:
                h_test = h.copy()
                h_test[i] = float(np.clip(h_test[i] + best_val, 0.0, 1.0))
                h_test[j] = float(np.clip(h_test[j] - best_val, 0.0, 1.0))
                h_test = project_feasible(h_test, n)
                C_new = compute_c5(h_test)
                if C_new < C - 1e-13:
                    h = h_test
                    C = C_new
                    improved_this_round = True

        if not improved_this_round:
            break

    return project_feasible(h, n), compute_c5(h)


def round_to_binary(h, n):
    if n % 2 != 0:
        return project_feasible(h, n)
    target = n // 2
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    hr = (h > 0.5).astype(float)
    cnt = int(np.sum(hr))
    if cnt > target:
        ones = np.where(hr == 1.0)[0]
        order = np.argsort(h[ones])
        for idx in ones[order[: cnt - target]]:
            hr[idx] = 0.0
    elif cnt < target:
        zeros = np.where(hr == 0.0)[0]
        order = np.argsort(-h[zeros])
        for idx in zeros[order[: target - cnt]]:
            hr[idx] = 1.0
    return project_feasible(hr, n)


def fourier_h_from_theta(theta, n, K):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    h = np.full(n, 0.5 + float(theta[0]))
    p = 1
    for m in range(1, K + 1):
        h += float(theta[p]) * np.cos(np.pi * m * x)
        h += float(theta[p + 1]) * np.sin(np.pi * m * x)
        p += 2
    return project_feasible(np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0), n)


def fourier_obj(theta, n, K, beta):
    h = fourier_h_from_theta(theta, n, K)
    corr = compute_corr(h)
    return smooth_max(corr, beta) * (2.0 / n)


def fit_fourier(h, n, K):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    dim = 1 + 2 * K
    B = np.ones((n, dim), dtype=float)
    p = 1
    for m in range(1, K + 1):
        B[:, p] = np.cos(np.pi * m * x)
        B[:, p + 1] = np.sin(np.pi * m * x)
        p += 2
    y = h - 0.5
    coef, _, _, _ = np.linalg.lstsq(B, y, rcond=None)
    return coef


def run_fourier_optimizer(h0, n, K, start, budget_s, seed):
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h)
    dim = 1 + 2 * K
    bnds = [(-0.12, 0.12)] + [(-0.75, 0.75)] * (dim - 1)

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    starts = [fit_fourier(h, n, K)]
    for _ in range(2):
        scale = np.zeros(dim, dtype=float)
        scale[0] = 0.04
        scale[1:] = 0.22
        starts.append(rng.normal(size=dim) * scale)

    for t in starts:
        if remaining_time(start, budget_s, 50) < 50:
            break
        t0 = np.array(t, dtype=float)
        t0 = np.clip(t0, np.array([b for b in bnds]), np.array([b[1] for b in bnds]))
        try:
            res = minimize(
                fourier_obj,
                t0,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                args=(n, K, 180),
                bounds=bnds,
                options={"maxiter": 90, "ftol": 1e-9, "maxls": 20, "maxcor": 8},
            )
        except Exception:
            continue
        if hasattr(res, "x") and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            h1 = project_feasible(fourier_h_from_theta(res.x, n, K), n)
            c1 = compute_c5(h1)
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_h = h1
                best_c = c1
    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.time()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_c = float("inf")
    best_n = 0

    try:
        parent = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
        if parent.size > 0:
            best_h = project_feasible(parent, len(parent))
            best_n = len(parent)
            best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    except Exception:
        best_h = None

    if best_h is None:
        best_n = 200
        best_h = make_random_h(best_n, rng)
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h)

    if remaining_time(start, budget_s, 80) > 80:
        h1, c1 = refine_with_slsqp(best_h, best_n, start, budget_s, heavy=True)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h1, c1

        if remaining_time(start, budget_s, 50) > 50:
            h2, c2 = polish_gradient(best_h, best_n, start, budget_s, beta=700, max_iter=130)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h2, c2

        if remaining_time(start, budget_s, 40) > 40:
            mp = 300 if best_n <= 250 else 180
            h3, c3 = local_pair_search(best_h, best_n, start, budget_s, max_rounds=8, max_pairs=mp)
            if c3 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h3, c3

    if remaining_time(start, budget_s, 150) > 150:
        n_fourier = best_n if 128 <= best_n <= 320 else 240
        hf, cf = run_fourier_optimizer(best_h, n_fourier, 5, start, budget_s, seed + 17)
        if cf < best_c:
            if n_fourier != best_n:
                hf = interp_resample(hf, best_n)
                cf = compute_c5(hf)
            if cf < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = hf, cf
            if remaining_time(start, budget_s, 40) > 40:
                h4, c4 = local_pair_search(best_h, best_n, start, budget_s, max_rounds=6, max_pairs=220)
                if c4 < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c = h4, c4

    base = best_n
    candidates = []
    for d in (0, -48, -24, 24, 48, 80, 128, 160, 208, 256):
        candidates.append(base + d)
    candidates.extend([160, 192, 224, 256, 288, 320, 352, 384])
    candidates = sorted(set([c for c in candidates if c % 2 == 0 and 112 <= c <= 384]))

    tried = 0
    for n in candidates:
        if n == best_n:
            continue
        if remaining_time(start, budget_s, 150) < 150:
            break
        if tried >= 3:
            break
        tried += 1

        h0 = interp_resample(best_h, n)
        h1, c1 = refine_with_slsqp(h0, n, start, budget_s, heavy=True)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = h1, c1, n

        if remaining_time(start, budget_s, 60) < 60:
            continue

        mp = 260 if n <= 250 else 160
        h2, c2 = local_pair_search(best_h, best_n, start, budget_s, max_rounds=6, max_pairs=mp)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h2, best_n

    if remaining_time(start, budget_s, 250) > 250:
        for _ in range(2):
            if remaining_time(start, budget_s, 200) < 200:
                break
            h0 = make_random_h(best_n, rng)
            h1, c1 = refine_with_slsqp(h0, best_n, start, budget_s, heavy=False)
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h1, c1
            if remaining_time(start, budget_s, 50) > 50:
                h2, c2 = local_pair_search(best_h, best_n, start, budget_s, max_rounds=5, max_pairs=180)
                if c2 < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c = h2, c2

    if best_n % 2 == 0 and remaining_time(start, budget_s, 90) > 90:
        hb = round_to_binary(best_h, best_n)
        cb = compute_c5(hb)
        if cb < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hb, cb
        if remaining_time(start, budget_s, 60) > 60:
            hb2, cb2 = local_pair_search(hb, best_n, start, budget_s, max_rounds=5, max_pairs=200)
            if cb2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = hb2, cb2

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    final_c = compute_c5(best_h)

    try:
        val = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
        if val is not None:
            vv = float(val)
            if np.isfinite(vv) and vv < final_c:
                final_c = vv
    except Exception:
        pass

    return best_h, float(final_c), int(best_n)
```
